cols = {}\n            for c in range(1, ws.max_column + 1):\n                pv = parse_int_phase(ws.cell(r, c).value)\n                if pv is not None and PHASE_START <= pv <= PHASE_END:\n {p} at ({label_row},{c})')\n            demand[p] = float(v)\n\n    missing = [p for p in range(PHASE_START, PHASE_END + 1) if p not in demand]\n    if missing:\n        raise RuntimeError(f'Demand missing for phases: {missing}')\n\n    return demand\n\n\ndef compute_plan(demand: dict[int, float]):\n    rows = []\n    prev_end_backlog = None\n\n    for p in range(PHASE_START, PHASE_END + 1):\n        if p == PHASE_START:\n            calc_start = 1138.66 - demand[p]\n        else:\n            assert prev_end_backlog is not None\n            calc_start = prev_end_backlog\n\n        past_due = max(0.0, calc_start)\n\n        # Choose days worked per instructions\n        if past_due > 0.01:\n            # Try 5 first\n            if calc_start + demand[p] - 35 * 5 <= 0:\n                days_worked = 5\n            elif calc_start + demand[p] - 35 * 6 <= 0:\n                days_worked = 6\n            else:\n                days_worked = 6\n        else:\n            if demand[p] <= weekly_capacity\n        overtime = 10.0 * max(0, days_worked - 4)\n\n        row = {\n            'Phase': p,\n            'Days Worked': days_worked,\n            'Scheduled Demand (Std Hrs)':          'Overtime Hours': overtime,\n            '_calc_start': calc_start,\n        }\n        rows.append(row)\n        prev_end_backlog = end_backlog\n\n    return rows\n\n\ndef
